步骤 5：生成部署树\n\n## 目标\n\n从 sources 目录拷贝脚本并生成可运行的多节点部署包结构。\n\n## 前置条件\n\n**必须先完成步骤 4**，确保以下文件存在于 `{output_dir}/sources/` 目录：\n\n| 文件 | 必须 | 来源 |\n|------|------|------|\n| `{model_name}.md` | ✓ | 步骤 2 输出目录命名\n\n格式：`multi_node_{model_normalized}_{N}nodes`\n\n示例：`multi_node_deepseek_v3_1_2nodes`\n\n**名称规范化**：\n- 将模型名称转换为小写\n- 将 `-` 和 `.` 替换为 `_`\n- 移除重复的下划线\n\n## 目录结构\n\n```text\n{output_dir}/\n├── ...\n└── README.md\n```\n\n## 生成步骤\n\n### 步骤 1：拷贝容器脚本到每个节点\n\n- 来源：`sources/start_container.sh`\n- 目标：`node{N}/start_container.sh`\n- 修改点（所有节点相同）：\n\n| 占位符 | 替换为 | 说明 |\n|---|---|---|\n| `{model_path}` | 用户提供的模型路径 | 模型权重目录 |\n| `{extra_mounts}` | 用户提供的挂载目录 | 额外挂载 |\n\n### 步骤 2：拷贝启动脚本到对应节点\n\n- Node 0：`sources/run_node0.sh` → `node0/run_serve.sh`\n- Node 1：`sources/run_node1.sh` → `node1/run_serve.sh`\n- Node 2：`sources/run_node2.sh` → `node2/run_serve.sh`（如有）\n- Node 3：`sources/run_node3.sh` → `node3/run_serve.sh`（如有）\n\n### 步骤 3：修改各节点启动脚本\n\n**Node 0 修改点**：\n\n| 占位符 | 替换为 | {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_tool-be237434676f4fb5bf32b580f8ed4835","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# DeepSeek-V3/3.1\n2\t\n3\t## Introduction\n4\t\n5\tDeepSeek-V3.1 is a hybrid model that supports both thinking mode and non-thinking mode. Compared to the previous version, this upgrade brings improvements in multiple aspects:\n6\t\n7\t- Hybrid thinking mode: One model supports both thinking mode and non-thinking mode by changing the chat template.\n8\t\n9\t- Smarter tool calling: Through post-training optimization, the model's performance in tool usage and agent tasks has significantly improved.\n10\t\n11\t- Higher thinking efficiency: DeepSeek-V3.1-Think achieves comparable answer quality to DeepSeek-R1-0528, while responding more quickly.\n12\t\n13\tThe `DeepSeek-V3.1` model is first supported in `vllm-ascend:v0.9.1rc3`.\n14\t\n15\tThis document will show the main verification steps of the model, including supported features, feature configuration, environment preparation, single-node and multi-node deployment, accuracy and performance evaluation.\n16\t\n17\t## Supported Features\n18\t\n19\tRefer to [supported features](../../user_guide/support_matrix/supported_models.md) to get the model's supported feature matrix.\n20\t\n21\tRefer to [feature guide](../../user_guide/feature_guide/index.md) to get the feature's configuration.\n22\t\n23\t## Environment Preparation\n24\t\n25\t### Model Weight\n26\t\n27\t- `DeepSeek-V3.1`(BF16 version): [Download model weight](https://www.modelscope.cn/models/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V3.1).\n28\t- `DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot`(Quantized version with mix mtp): [Download model weight](https://www.modelscope.cn/models/Eco-Tech/DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot).\n29\t- `DeepSeek-V3.1-Terminus-w4a8-mtp-QuaRot`(Quantized version with mix mtp): [Download model weight](https://www.modelscope.cn/models/Eco-Tech/DeepSeek-V3.1-Terminus-w4a8-mtp-QuaRot).\n30\t- `Quantization method`: [msmodelslim](https://gitcode.com/Ascend/msit/blob/master/msmodelslim/example/DeepSeek/README.md#deepseek-v31-w8a8-%E6%B7%B7%E5%90%88%E9%87%8F%E5%8C%96-mtp-%E9%87%8F%E5%8C%96). You can use this method to quantize the model.\n31\t\n32\tIt is recommended to download the model weight to the shared directory of multiple nodes, such as `/root/.cache/`.\n33\t\n34\t### Verify Multi-node Communication(Optional)\n35\t\n36\tIf you want to deploy multi-node environment, you need to verify multi-node communication according to [verify multi-node communication environment](../../installation.md#verify-multi-node-communication).\n37\t\n38\t### Installation\n39\t\n40\tYou can use our official docker image to run `DeepSeek-V3.1` directly.\n41\t\n42\tSelect an image based on your machine type and start the docker image on your node, refer to [using docker](../../installation.md#set-up-using-docker).\n43\t\n44\t```{code-block} bash\n45\t   :substitutions:\n46\t# Update --device according to your device (Atlas A2: /dev/davinci[0-7] Atlas A3:/dev/davinci[0-15]).\n47\t# Update the vllm-ascend image according to your environment.\n48\t# Note you should download the weight to /root/.cache in advance.\n49\t# Update the vllm-ascend image\n50\texport IMAGE=m.daocloud.io/quay.io/ascend/vllm-ascend:|vllm_ascend_version|\n51\texport NAME=vllm-ascend\n52\t\n53\t# Run the container using the defined variables\n54\t# Note: If you are running bridge network with docker, please expose available ports for multiple nodes communication in advance.\n55\tdocker run --rm \\\n56\t--name $NAME \\\n57\t--net=host \\\n58\t--shm-size=1g \\\n59\t--device /dev/davinci0 \\\n60\t--device /dev/davinci1 \\\n61\t--device /dev/davinci2 \\\n62\t--device /dev/davinci3 \\\n63\t--device /dev/davinci4 \\\n64\t--device /dev/davinci5 \\\n65\t--device /dev/davinci6 \\\n66\t--device /dev/davinci7 \\\n67\t--device /dev/davinci_manager \\\n68\t--device /dev/devmm_svm \\\n69\t--device /dev/hisi_hdc \\\n70\t-v /usr/local/dcmi:/usr/local/dcmi \\\n71\t-v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/tools/hccn_tool:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/tools/hccn_tool \\\n72\t-v /usr/local/bin/npu-smi:/usr/local/bin/npu-smi \\\n73\t-v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/lib64/:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/lib64/ \\\n74\t-v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/version.info:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/version.info \\\n75\t-v /etc/ascend_install.info:/etc/ascend_install.info \\\n76\t-v /root/.cache:/root/.cache \\\n77\t-it $IMAGE bash\n78\t```\n79\t\n80\tIf you want to deploy multi-node environment, you need to set up environment on each node.\n81\t\n82\t## Deployment\n83\t\n84\t### Single-node Deployment\n85\t\n86\t- Quantized model `DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot` can be deployed on 1 Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16).\n87\t\n88\tRun the following script to execute online inference.\n89\t\n90\t```shell\n91\t#!/bin/sh\n92\t# this obtained through ifconfig\n93\t# nic_name is the network interface name corresponding to local_ip of the current node\n94\tnic_name=\"xxxx\"\n95\tlocal_ip=\"xxxx\"\n96\t\n97\t# [Optional] jemalloc\n98\t# jemalloc is for better performance, if `libjemalloc.so` is installed on your machine, you can turn it on.\n99\t# export LD_PRELOAD=/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu/libjemalloc.so.2:$LD_PRELOAD\n100\t\n101\t# AIV\n102\texport HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE=\"AIV\"\n103\t\n104\texport HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip\n105\texport GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\n106\texport TP_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\n107\texport HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\n108\texport VLLM_ASCEND_BALANCE_SCHEDULING=1\n109\texport PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\n110\t\n111\tvllm serve /weights/DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot \\\n112\t--host 0.0.0.0 \\\n113\t--port 8015 \\\n114\t--data-parallel-size 4 \\\n115\t--tensor-parallel-size 4 \\\n116\t--quantization ascend \\\n117\t--seed 1024 \\\n118\t--served-model-name deepseek_v3 \\\n119\t--enable-expert-parallel \\\n120\t--async-scheduling \\\n121\t--max-num-seqs 16 \\\n122\t--max-model-len 16384 \\\n123\t--max-num-batched-tokens 4096 \\\n124\t--trust-remote-code \\\n125\t--no-enable-prefix-caching \\\n126\t--gpu-memory-utilization 0.92 \\\n127\t--speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 3, \"method\": \"mtp\"}' \\\n128\t--compilation-config '{\"cudagraph_capture_sizes\":[4,16,32,48,64], \"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\"}'\n129\t```\n130\t\n131\t**Notice:**\n132\tThe parameters are explained as follows:\n133\t\n134\t- Setting the environment variable `VLLM_ASCEND_BALANCE_SCHEDULING=1` enables balance scheduling. This may help increase output throughput and reduce TPOT in v1 scheduler. However, TTFT may degrade in some scenarios. Furthermore, enabling this feature is not recommended in scenarios where PD is separated.\n135\t- For single-node deployment, we recommend using `dp4tp4` instead of `dp2tp8`.\n136\t- `--max-model-len` specifies the maximum context length - that is, the sum of input and output tokens for a single request. For performance testing with an input length of 3.5K and output length of 1.5K, a value of `16384` is sufficient, however, for precision testing, please set it at least `35000`.\n137\t- `--no-enable-prefix-caching` indicates that prefix caching is disabled. To enable it, remove this option.\n138\t- If you use the w4a8 weight, more memory will be allocated to kvcache, and you can try to increase system throughput to achieve greater throughput.\n139\t\n140\t### Multi-node Deployment\n141\t\n142\t- `DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot`: require at least 2 Atlas 800 A2 (64G × 8).\n143\t\n144\tRun the following scripts on two nodes respectively.\n145\t\n146\t**Node 0**\n147\t\n148\t```shell\n149\t#!/bin/sh\n150\t\n151\t# this obtained through ifconfig\n152\t# nic_name is the network interface name corresponding to local_ip of the current node\n153\tnic_name=\"xxxx\"\n154\tlocal_ip=\"xxxx\"\n155\t\n156\t# The value of node0_ip must be consistent with the value of local_ip set in node0 (master node)\n157\tnode0_ip=\"xxxx\"\n158\t\n159\t# [Optional] jemalloc\n160\t# jemalloc is for better performance, if `libjemalloc.so` is installed on your machine, you can turn it on.\n161\t# export LD_PRELOAD=/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu/libjemalloc.so.2:$LD_PRELOAD\n162\t\n163\texport HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip\n164\texport GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\n165\texport TP_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\n166\texport HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\n167\texport OMP_PROC_BIND=false\n168\texport OMP_NUM_THREADS=1\n169\texport HCCL_BUFFSIZE=200\n170\texport PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\n171\texport VLLM_ASCEND_BALANCE_SCHEDULING=1\n172\texport HCCL_INTRA_PCIE_ENABLE=1\n173\texport HCCL_INTRA_ROCE_ENABLE=0\n174\t\n175\tvllm serve /weights/DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot \\\n176\t--host 0.0.0.0 \\\n177\t--port 8004 \\\n178\t--data-parallel-size 4 \\\n179\t--data-parallel-size-local 2 \\\n180\t--data-parallel-address $node0_ip \\\n181\t--data-parallel-rpc-port 13389 \\\n182\t--tensor-parallel-size 4 \\\n183\t--quantization ascend \\\n184\t--seed 1024 \\\n185\t--served-model-name deepseek_v3 \\\n186\t--enable-expert-parallel \\\n187\t--async-scheduling \\\n188\t--max-num-seqs 16 \\\n189\t--max-model-len 16384 \\\n190\t--max-num-batched-tokens 4096 \\\n191\t--trust-remote-code \\\n192\t--no-enable-prefix-caching \\\n193\t--gpu-memory-utilization 0.92 \\\n194\t--speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 3, \"method\": \"mtp\"}' \\\n195\t--compilation-config '{\"cudagraph_capture_sizes\":[4,16,32,48,64], \"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\"}'\n196\t```\n197\t\n198\t**Node 1**\n199\t\n200\t```shell\n201\t#!/bin/sh\n202\t\n203\t# this obtained through ifconfig\n204\t# nic_name is the network node0 (master node)\n209\tnode0_ip=\"xxxx\"\n210\t\n211\t# [Optional] jemalloc\n212\t# jemalloc is for better performance, if `libjemalloc.so` is installed on your machine, you can turn it on.\n213\t# export LD_PRELOAD=/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu/libjemalloc.so.2:$LD_PRELOAD\n214\t\n215\texport HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip\n216\texport GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\n217\texport /weights/DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot \\\n228\t--host 0.0.0.0 \\\n229\t--port 8004 \\\n230\t--headless \\\n231\t--data-parallel-size 4 \\\n232\t--data-parallel-size-local 2 \\\n233\t--data-parallel-start-rank 2 \\\n234\t--data-parallel-address $node0_ip \\\n235\t--data-parallel-rpc-port 13389 \\\n236\t--tensor-parallel-size 4 \\\n237\t--quantization ascend \\\n238\t--seed 1024 \\\n239\t--served-model-name deepseek_v3 \\\n240\t--enable-expert-parallel \\\n241\t--async-scheduling \\\n242\t--max-num-seqs 16 \\\n243\t--max-model-len 16384 \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\"}'\n250\t```\n251\t\n252\t### Prefill-Decode Disaggregation\n253\t\n254\tWe recommend using Mooncake for deployment: [Mooncake](../features/pd_disaggregation_mooncake_multi_node.md).\n255\t\n256\tTake Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16) for example, we recommend to deploy 2P1D (4 nodes) rather than 1P1D (2 nodes), because there is no enough NPU memory to serve high concurrency in 1P1D case.\n257\t\n258\t- `DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot 2P1D Layerwise` require 4 Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16).\n259\t\n260\tTo run the vllm-ascend `Prefill-Decode Disaggregation` service, you need to deploy a `launch_online_dp.py` script and a `run_dp_template.sh` script on each node and deploy a `proxy.sh` script on prefill master node to forward requests.\n261\t\n262\t1. `launch_online_dp.py` to launch external dp vllm servers.\n263\t[launch\\_online\\_dp.py](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm-ascend/blob/main/examples/external_online_dp/launch_online_dp.py)\n264\t\n265\t2. Prefill Node 0 `run_dp_template.sh` script\n266\t\n267\t    ```shell\n268\t    # this obtained through ifconfig\n269\t    # nic_name is the network interface name corresponding to local_ip of the current node\n270\t    nic_name=\"xxx\"\n271\t    local_ip=\"141.xx.xx.1\"\n272\t\n273\t    # The value of node0_ip must be consistent with the value of local_ip set in node0 (master node)\n274\t    node0_ip=\"xxxx\"\n275\t\n276\t    # [Optional] jemalloc\n277\t    # jemalloc is for better performance, if `libjemalloc.so` is installed on your machine, you can turn it on.\n278\t    # export LD_PRELOAD=/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu/libjemalloc.so.2:$LD_PRELOAD\n279\t\n280\t    export HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip\n281\t    export GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\n282\t    export TP_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\n283\t    export HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\n284\t\n285\t    export VLLM_RPC_TIMEOUT=3600000\n286\t    export VLLM_EXECUTE_MODEL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=30000\n287\t    export HCCL_EXEC_TIMEOUT=204\n288\t    export HCCL_CONNECT_TIMEOUT=120\n289\t\n290\t    export OMP_PROC_BIND=false\n291\t    export OMP_NUM_THREADS=10\n292\t    export LD_LIBRARY_PATH=/usr/local/Ascend/ascend-toolkit/latest/python/site-packages/mooncake:$LD_LIBRARY_PATH\n300\t\n301\t    export VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_FLASHCOMM1=1\n302\t\n303\t    vllm serve /weights/DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot \\\n304\t      --host 0.0.0.0 \\\n305\t      --port $2 \\\n306\t      --data-parallel-size $3 \\\n307\t      --data-parallel-rank $4 \\\n308\t      --data-parallel-address $5 \\\n309\t      --data-parallel-rpc-port $6 \\\n310\t      --tensor-parallel-size $7 \\\n311\t      --enable-expert-parallel \\\n312\t      --seed 1024 \\\n313\t      --served-model-name deepseek_v3 \\\n314\t      --max-model-len 65536 \\\n315\t      --max-num-batched-tokens 16384 \\\n316\t      --max-num-seqs 8 \\\n317\t      --enforce-eager \\\n318\t      --trust-remote-code \\\n319\t      --gpu-memory-utilization 0.9 \\\n320\t      --quantization ascend \\\n321\t      --no-enable-prefix-caching \\\n322\t      --speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 1, \"method\": \"mtp\"}' \\\n323\t      --additional-config '{\"recompute_scheduler_enable\":true}' \\\n324\t      --kv-transfer-config \\\n325\t      '{\"kv_connector\": \"MooncakeConnectorV1\",\n326\t      \"kv_role\": \"kv_producer\",\n327\t      \"kv_port\": \"30000\",\n328\t      \"engine_id\": \"0\",\n329\t      \"kv_connector_extra_config\": {\n330\t                     \"dp_size\": 32,\n565\t                        \"tp_size\": 1\n566\t                }\n567\t          }\n568\t      }'\n569\t    ```\n570\t\n571\t    **Notice:**\n572\t    The parameters are explained as follows:\n573\t\n574\t    - `VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_FLASHCOMM1=1`: enables the communication optimization function on the prefill nodes.\n575\t    - `VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_MLAPO=1`: enables the fusion operator, which can significantly improve performance but consumes more NPU memory. In the Prefill-Decode (PD) separation scenario, enable MLAPO only on decode nodes.\n576\t    - `--async-scheduling`: enables the asynchronous scheduling function. When Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) is enabled, asynchronous scheduling of operator delivery can be implemented to overlap the operator delivery latency.\n577\t    - `cudagraph_capture_sizes`: The recommended value is `n x (mtp + 1)`. And the min is `n = 1` and the max is `n = max-num-seqs`. For other values, it is recommended to set them to the number of frequently occurring requests on the Decode (D) node.\n578\t    - `recompute_scheduler_enable: true`: enables the recomputation scheduler. When the Key-Value Cache (KV Cache) of the decode node is insufficient, requests will be sent to the prefill node to recompute the KV Cache. In the PD separation scenario, it is recommended to enable this configuration on both prefill and decode nodes simultaneously.\n579\t    - `multistream_overlap_shared_expert: true`: When the Tensor Parallelism (TP) size is 1 or `enable_shared_expert_dp: true`, an additional stream is enabled to overlap the computation process of shared experts for improved efficiency.\n580\t    - `lmhead_tensor_parallel_size: 16`: When the Tensor Parallelism (TP) size of the decode node is 1, this parameter allows the TP size of the LMHead embedding layer to be greater than 1, which is used to reduce the computational load of each card on the LMHead embedding layer.\n581\t\n582\t6. run server for each node:\n583\t\n584\t    ```shell\n585\t    # p0\n586\t    python launch_online_dp.py --dp-size 2 --tp-size 8 --dp-size-local 2 --dp-rank-start 0 --dp-address 141.xx.xx.1 --dp-rpc-port 12321 --vllm-start-port 7100\n587\t    # p1\n588\t    python launch_online_dp.py --dp-size 2 --tp-size 8 --dp-size-local 2 --dp-rank-start 0 --dp-address 141.xx.xx.2 --dp-rpc-port 12321 --vllm-start-port 7100\n589\t    # d0\n590\t    python launch_online_dp.py --dp-size 32 --tp-size 1 --dp-size-local 16 --dp-rank-start 0 --dp-address 141.xx.xx.3 --dp-rpc-port 12321 --vllm-start-port 7100\n591\t    # d1\n592\t    python launch_online_dp.py --dp-size 32 --tp-size 1 --dp-size-local 16 --dp-rank-start 16 --dp-address 141.xx.xx.3 --dp-rpc-port 12321 --vllm-start-port 7100\n593\t    ```\n594\t\n595\t7. Run the code as follows.\n716\t\n717\t```shell\n718\tvllm bench serve --model /weights/DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot  --dataset-name random --random-input 1024 --num-prompts 200 --request-rate 1 --save-result --result-dir ./\n719\t```\n720\t\n721\tAfter about several minutes, you can get the performance evaluation result."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6af604a1-3367-4e7e-8601-75667577863f","uuid":"19fc9f5d-a48c-441d-9110-0bb1031142c4","timestamp":"2026-05-23T03:57:25.712Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"D:\\chenyu\\code\\Gitee\\hw_ascend_study\\code\\skills\\dev\\tests\\vllm-ascend-multi-node-zh\\scenarios\\deepseek-v3-1\\baseline\\multi_node_deepseek_v3_1_2nodes\\sources\\DeepSeek-V3.1.md","content":"# DeepSeek-V3/3.1\n\n## Introduction\n\nDeepSeek-V3.1 is a hybrid model that supports both thinking mode and non-thinking mode. Compared to the previous version, this upgrade brings improvements in multiple aspects:\n\n- Hybrid thinking mode: One model supports both thinking mode and non-thinking mode by changing the chat template.\n\n- Smarter tool calling: Through post-training optimization, the model's performance in tool usage and agent tasks has significantly improved.\n\n- Higher thinking efficiency: DeepSeek-V3.1-Think achieves comparable answer quality to DeepSeek-R1-0528, while responding more quickly.\n\nThe `DeepSeek-V3.1` model is first supported in `vllm-ascend:v0.9.1rc3`.\n\nThis document will show the main verification steps of the model, including supported features, feature configuration, environment preparation, single-node and multi-node deployment, accuracy and performance evaluation.\n\n## Supported Features\n\nRefer to [supported features](../../user_guide/support_matrix/supported_models.md) to get the model's supported feature matrix.\n\nRefer to [feature guide](../../user_guide/feature_guide/index.md) to get the feature's configuration.\n\n## Environment Preparation\n\n### Model Weight\n\n- `DeepSeek-V3.1`(BF16 version): [Download model weight](https://www.modelscope.cn/models/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V3.1).\n- `DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot`(Quantized version with mix mtp): [Download model weight](https://www.modelscope.cn/models/Eco-Tech/DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot).\n- `DeepSeek-V3.1-Terminus-w4a8-mtp-QuaRot`(Quantized version with mix mtp): [Download model weight](https://www.modelscope.cn/models/Eco-Tech/DeepSeek-V3.1-Terminus-w4a8-mtp-QuaRot).\n- `Quantization method`: [msmodelslim](https://gitcode.com/Ascend/msit/blob/master/msmodelslim/example/DeepSeek/README.md#deepseek-v31-w8a8-%E6%B7%B7%E5%90%88%E9%87%8F%E5%8C%96-mtp-%E9%87%8F%E5%8C%96). You can use this method to quantize the model.\n\nIt is recommended to download the model weight to the shared directory of multiple nodes, such as `/root/.cache/`.\n\n### Verify Multi-node Communication(Optional)\n\nIf you want to deploy multi-node environment, you need to verify multi-node communication according to [verify multi-node communication environment](../../installation.md#verify-multi-node-communication).\n\n### Installation\n\nYou can use our official docker image to run `DeepSeek-V3.1` directly.\n\nSelect an image based on your machine type and start the docker image on your node, refer to [using docker](../../installation.md#set-up-using-docker).\n\n```{code-block} bash\n   :substitutions:\n# Update --device according to your device (Atlas A2: /dev/davinci[0-7] Atlas A3:/dev/davinci[0-15]).\n# Update the vllm-ascend image according to your environment.\n# Note you should download the weight to /root/.cache in advance.\n# Update the vllm-ascend image\nexport IMAGE=m.daocloud.io/quay.io/ascend/vllm-ascend:|vllm_ascend_version|\nexport NAME=vllm-ascend\n\n# Run the container using the defined variables\n# Note: If you are running bridge network with docker, please expose available ports for multiple nodes communication in advance.\ndocker run --rm \\\n--name $NAME \\\n--net=host \\\n--shm-size=1g \\\n--device /dev/davinci0 \\\n--device /dev/davinci1 \\\n--device /dev/davinci2 \\\n--device /dev/davinci3 \\\n--device /dev/davinci4 \\\n--device /dev/davinci5 \\\n--device /dev/davinci6 \\\n--device /dev/davinci7 \\\n--device /dev/davinci_manager \\\n--device /dev/devmm_svm \\\n--device /dev/hisi_hdc \\\n-v /usr/local/dcmi:/usr/local/dcmi \\\n-v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/tools/hccn_tool:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/tools/hccn_tool \\\n-v /usr/local/bin/npu-smi:/usr/local/bin/npu-smi \\\n-v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/lib64/:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/lib64/ \\\n-v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/version.info:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/version.info \\\n-v /etc/ascend_install.info:/etc/ascend_install.info \\\n-v /root/.cache:/root/.cache \\\n-it $IMAGE bash\n```\n\nIf you want to deploy multi-node environment, you need to set up environment on each node.\n\n## Deployment\n\n### Single-node Deployment\n\n- Quantized model `DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot` can be deployed on 1 Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16).\n\nRun the following script to execute online inference.\n\n```shell\n#!/bin/sh\n# this obtained through ifconfig\n# nic_name is the network interface name corresponding to local_ip of the current node\nnic_name=\"xxxx\"\nlocal_ip=\"xxxx\"\n\n# [Optional] jemalloc\n# jemalloc is for better performance, if `libjemalloc.so` is installed on your machine, you can turn it on.\n# export LD_PRELOAD=/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu/libjemalloc.so.2:$LD_PRELOAD\n\n# AIV\nexport HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE=\"AIV\"\n\nexport HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip\nexport GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport TP_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_BALANCE_SCHEDULING=1\nexport PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\n\nvllm serve /weights/DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot \\\n--host 0.0.0.0 \\\n--port 8015 \\\n--data-parallel-size 4 \\\n--tensor-parallel-size 4 \\\n--quantization ascend \\\n--seed 1024 \\\n--served-model-name deepseek_v3 \\\n--enable-expert-parallel \\\n--async-scheduling \\\n--max-num-seqs 16 \\\n--max-model-len 16384 \\\n--max-num-batched-tokens 4096 \\\n--trust-remote-code \\\n--no-enable-prefix-caching \\\n--gpu-memory-utilization 0.92 \\\n--speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 3, \"method\": \"mtp\"}' \\\n--compilation-config '{\"cudagraph_capture_sizes\":[4,16,32,48,64], \"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\"}'\n```\n\n**Notice:**\nThe parameters are explained as follows:\n\n- Setting the environment variable `VLLM_ASCEND_BALANCE_SCHEDULING=1` enables balance scheduling. This may help increase output throughput and reduce TPOT in v1 scheduler. However, TTFT may degrade in some scenarios. Furthermore, enabling this feature is not recommended in scenarios where PD is separated.\n- For single-node deployment, we recommend using `dp4tp4` instead of `dp2tp8`.\n- `--max-model-len` specifies the maximum context length - that is, the sum of input and output tokens for a single request. For performance testing with an input length of 3.5K and output length of 1.5K, a value of `16384` is sufficient, however, for precision testing, please set it at least `35000`.\n- `--no-enable-prefix-caching` indicates that prefix caching is disabled. To enable it, remove this option.\n- If you use the w4a8 weight, more memory will be allocated to kvcache, and you can try to increase system throughput to achieve greater throughput.\n\n### Multi-node Deployment\n\n- `DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot`: require at least 2 Atlas 800 A2 (64G × 8).\n\nRun the following scripts on two nodes respectively.\n\n**Node 0**\n\n```shell\n#!/bin/sh\n\n# you can turn it on.\n# export LD_PRELOAD=/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu/libjemalloc.so.2:$LD_PRELOAD\n\nexport HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip\nexport GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport \"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\"}'\n```\n\n### Prefill-Decode Disaggregation\n\nWe recommend using Mooncake for deployment: [Mooncake](../features/pd_disaggregation_mooncake_multi_node.md).\n\nTake Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16) for example, we recommend to deploy 2P1D (4 nodes) rather than 1P1D (2 nodes), because there is no enough NPU memory to serve high concurrency in 1P1D case.\n\n- `DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot 2P1D Layerwise` require 4 Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16).\n\nTo run the vllm-ascend `Prefill-Decode Disaggregation` service, you need to deploy a `launch_online_dp.py` script and a `run_dp_template.sh` script on each node and deploy a `proxy.sh` script on prefill master node to forward requests.\n\n1. `launch_online_dp.py` to launch external dp vllm servers.\n[launch\\_online\\_dp.py](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm-ascend/blob/main/examples/external_online_dp/launch_online_dp.py)\n\n2. Prefill Node 0 `run_dp_template.sh` script\n\n    ```shell\n    # this obtained through ifconfig\n    # nic_name is the network interface name corresponding to local_ip of the current node\n    nic_name=\"xxx\"\n    local_ip=\"141.xx.xx.1\"\n\n    # The value of node0_ip must be consistent with the value of local_ip set in node0 (master node)\n    node0_ip=\"xxxx\"\n\n    # [Optional] Decode Node 1 `run_dp_template.sh` script\n\n    ```shell\n    # this obtained through ifconfig\n    # nic_name is the network interface name corresponding to local_ip of the current node\n    nic_name=\"xxx\"\n    local_ip=\"141.xx.xx.4\"\n\n    # The value of node0_ip must be consistent with the value of local_ip set in node0 (master node)\n    node0_ip=\"xxxx\"\n\n    # [Optional] jemalloc\n    # jemalloc is for better performance, if `libjemalloc.so` is installed on your machine, you can turn it on.\n    # export HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\n\n    export VLLM_RPC_TIMEOUT=3600000\n    export VLLM_EXECUTE_MODEL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=30000\n    export HCCL_EXEC_TIMEOUT=204\n    export HCCL_CONNECT_TIMEOUT=120\n\n    export OMP_PROC_BIND=false\n    export OMP_NUM_THREADS=10\n    export PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\n    export HCCL_BUFFSIZE=1100\n    export TASK_QUEUE_ENABLE=1\n    export HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE=\"AIV\"\n    export VLLM_USE_V1=1\n    export ASCEND_RT_VISIBLE_DEVICES=$1\n    export ASCEND_BUFFER_POOL=4:8\n    export LD_LIBRARY_PATH=/usr/local/Ascend/ascend-toolkit/latest/python/site-packages/mooncake:$LD_LIBRARY_PATH\n\n    vllm serve /weights/DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot \\\n      --host 0.0.0.0 \\\n --enable-expert-parallel \\\n      --seed 1024 \\\n      --served-model-name deepseek_v3 \\\n      --max-model-len 65536 \\\n      --max-num-batched-tokens 256 \\\n      --max-num-seqs 28 \\\n      --trust-remote-code \\\n      --gpu-memory-utilization 0.92 \\\n      --quantization ascend \\\n      --no-enable-prefix-caching \\\n      --async-scheduling \\\n      --speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 1, \"method\": \"mtp\"}' \\\n      --compilation-config '{\"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\", \"cudagraph_capture_sizes\":[2, 4, 8, 16, 24, 32, 48, 56]}' \\\n      --additional-config '{\"recompute_scheduler_enable\":true,\"multistream_overlap_shared_expert\": true,\"finegrained_tp_config\": {\"lmhead_tensor_parallel_size\":16}}' \\\n      --kv-transfer-config \\\n      '{\"kv_connector\": \"MooncakeConnectorV1\",\n      \"kv_role\": \"kv_consumer\",\n      \"kv_port\": \"30200\",\n      \"engine_id\": \"2\",\n      \"kv_connector_extra_config\": {\n                \"prefill\": {\n                        \"dp_size\": 2,\n                        \"tp_size\": 8\n                },\n                \"decode\": {\n                        \"dp_size\": 32,\n                        \"tp_size\": 1\n                }\n          }\n      }'\n    ```\n\n    **Notice:**\n    The parameters are explained as follows:\n\n    - `VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_FLASHCOMM1=1`: enables the communication optimization function on the prefill nodes.\n    - `VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_MLAPO=1`: enables the fusion operator, which can significantly improve performance but consumes more NPU memory. In the Prefill-Decode (PD) separation scenario, enable MLAPO only on decode nodes.\n    - `--async-scheduling`: enables the asynchronous scheduling function. When Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) is enabled, asynchronous scheduling of operator delivery can be implemented to overlap the operator delivery latency.\n    - `cudagraph_capture_sizes`: The recommended value is `n x (mtp + 1)`. And the min is `n = 1` and the max is `n = max-num-seqs`. For other values, it is recommended to set them to the number of frequently occurring requests on the Decode (D) node.\n    - `recompute_scheduler_enable: true`: enables the recomputation scheduler. When the Key-Value Cache (KV Cache) of the decode node is insufficient, requests will be sent to the prefill node to recompute the KV Cache. In the PD separation scenario, it is recommended to enable this configuration on both prefill and decode nodes simultaneously.\n    - `multistream_overlap_shared_expert: true`: When the Tensor Parallelism (TP) size is 1 or `enable_shared_expert_dp: true`, an additional stream is enabled to overlap the computation process of shared experts for improved efficiency.\n    - `lmhead_tensor_parallel_size: 16`: When the Tensor Parallelism (TP) size of the decode node is 1, this parameter allows the TP size of the LMHead embedding layer to be greater than 1, which is used to reduce the computational load of each card on the LMHead embedding layer.\n\n6. run server for each node:\n\n    ```shell\n    # p0\n    python launch_online_dp.py --dp-size 2 --tp-size 8 --dp-size-local 2 --dp-rank-start 0 --dp-address 141.xx.xx.1 --dp-rpc-port 12321 --vllm-start-port 7100\n    # p1\n    python launch_online_dp.py --dp-size 2 --tp-size 8 --dp-size-local 2 --dp-rank-start 0 --dp-address 141.xx.xx.2 --dp-rpc-port 12321 --vllm-start-port 7100\n    # d0\n    python launch_online_dp.py --dp-size 32 --tp-size 1 --dp-size-local 16 --dp-rank-start 0 --dp-address 141.xx.xx.3 --dp-rpc-port 12321 --vllm-start-port 7100\n    # d1\n    python launch_online_dp.py --dp-size 32 --tp-size 1 --dp-size-local 16 --dp-rank-start 16 --dp-address 141.xx.xx.3 --dp-rpc-port 12321 --vllm-start-port 7100\n    ```\n\n7. Run the `proxy.sh` script on the prefill master node\n\n    Run a proxy server on the same node with the prefiller service instance. You can get the proxy program in the repository's examples: [load\\_balance\\_proxy\\_server\\_example.py](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm-ascend/blob/main/examples/disaggregated_prefill_v1/load_balance_proxy_server_example.py)\n\n    ```shell\n    python load_balance_proxy_server_example.py \\\n      --port 1999 \\\n      --host 141.xx.xx.1 \\\n      --prefiller-hosts \\\n        141.xx.xx.1 \\\n        141.xx.xx.1 \\\n        141.xx.xx.2 \\\n        141.xx.xx.2 \\\n      --prefiller-ports \\\n        7100 7101 7100 7101 \\\n      --decoder-hosts \\\n        141.xx.xx.3 \\\n        141.xx.xx.3 \\\n        141.xx.xx.3 \\\n        141.xx.xx.3 \\\n        141.xx.xx.3 \\\n        141.xx.xx.3 \\\n        141.xx.xx.3 \\\n        141.xx.xx.3 \\\n        141.xx.xx.3 \\\n        141.xx.xx.3 \\\n        141.xx.xx.3 \\\n        141.xx.xx.3 \\\n        141.xx.xx.3 \\\n        141.xx.xx.3 \\\n        141.xx.xx.3 \\\n        141.xx.xx.3 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"glm-5","id":"msg_fea739bf-d980-4feb-82c8-b896398a9b59","role":"assistant","type":"message","content":[{"name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"D:\\chenyu\\code\\Gitee\\hw_ascend_study\\code\\skills\\dev\\tests\\vllm-ascend-multi-node-zh\\scenarios\\deepseek-v3-1\\output\\multi_node_deepseek_v3_1_2nodes\\sources\\DeepSeek-V3.1.md","content":"# DeepSeek-V3/3.1\n\n## Introduction\n\nDeepSeek-V3.1 is a hybrid model that supports both thinking mode and non-thinking mode. Compared to the previous version, this upgrade brings improvements in multiple aspects:\n\n- Hybrid thinking mode: One model supports both thinking mode and non-thinking mode by changing the chat template.\n\n- Smarter tool calling: Through post-training optimization, the model's performance in tool usage and agent tasks has significantly improved.\n\n- Higher thinking efficiency: DeepSeek-V3.1-Think achieves comparable answer quality to DeepSeek-R1-0528, while responding more quickly.\n\nThe `DeepSeek-V3.1` model is first supported in `vllm-ascend:v0.9.1rc3`.\n\nThis document will show the main verification steps of the model, including supported features, feature configuration, environment preparation, single-node and multi-node deployment, accuracy and performance evaluation.\n\n## Supported Features\n\nRefer to [supported features](../../user_guide/support_matrix/supported_models.md) to get the model's supported feature matrix.\n\nRefer to [feature guide](../../user_guide/feature_guide/index.md) to get the feature's configuration.\n\n## Environment Preparation\n\n### Model Weight\n\n- `DeepSeek-V3.1`(BF16 version): [Download model weight](https://www.modelscope.cn/models/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V3.1).\n- `DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot`(Quantized version with mix mtp): [Download model weight](https://www.modelscope.cn/models/Eco-Tech/DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot).\n- `DeepSeek-V3.1-Terminus-w4a8-mtp-QuaRot`(Quantized version with mix mtp): [Download model weight](https://www.modelscope.cn/models/Eco-Tech/DeepSeek-V3.1-Terminus-w4a8-mtp-QuaRot).\n- `Quantization method`: [msmodelslim](https://gitcode.com/Ascend/msit/blob/master/msmodelslim/example/DeepSeek/README.md#deepseek-v31-w8a8-%E6%B7%B7%E5%90%88%E9%87%8F%E5%8C%96-mtp-%E9%87%8F%E5%8C%96). You can use this method to quantize the model.\n\nIt is recommended to download the model weight to the shared directory of multiple nodes, such as `/root/.cache/`.\n\n### Verify Multi-node Communication(Optional)\n\nIf you want to deploy multi-node environment, you need to verify multi-node communication according to [verify multi-node communication environment](../../installation.md#verify-multi-node-communication).\n\n### Installation\n\nYou can use our official docker image to run `DeepSeek-V3.1` directly.\n\nSelect an image based on your machine type and start the docker image on your node, refer to [using docker](../../installation.md#set-up-using-docker).\n\n```{code-block} bash\n   :substitutions:\n# Update --device according to your device (Atlas A2: /dev/davinci[0-7] Atlas A3:/dev/davinci[0-15]).\n# Update the vllm-ascend image according to your environment.\n# Note you should download the weight to /root/.cache in advance.\n# Update the vllm-ascend image\nexport IMAGE=m.daocloud.io/quay.io/ascend/vllm-ascend:|vllm_ascend_version|\nexport NAME=vllm-ascend\n\n# Run the container using the defined variables\n# Note: If you are running bridge network with docker, please expose available ports for multiple nodes communication in advance.\ndocker run --rm \\\n--name $NAME \\\n--net=host \\\n--shm-size=1g \\\n--device /dev/davinci0 \\\n--device /dev/davinci1 \\\n--device /dev/davinci2 \\\n--device /dev/davinci3 \\\n--device /dev/davinci4 \\\n--device /dev/davinci5 \\\n--device /dev/davinci6 \\\n--device /dev/davinci7 \\\n--device /dev/davinci_manager \\\n--device /dev/devmm_svm \\\n--device /dev/hisi_hdc \\\n-v /usr/local/dcmi:/usr/local/dcmi \\\n-v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/tools/hccn_tool:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/tools/hccn_tool \\\n-v /usr/local/bin/npu-smi:/usr/local/bin/npu-smi \\\n-v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/lib64/:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/lib64/ \\\n-v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/version.info:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/version.info \\\n-v /etc/ascend_install.info:/etc/ascend_install.info \\\n-v /root/.cache:/root/.cache \\\n-it $IMAGE bash\n```\n\nIf you want to deploy multi-node environment, you need to set up environment on each node.\n\n## Deployment\n\n### Single-node Deployment\n\n- Quantized model `DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot` can be deployed on 1 Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16).\n\nRun the following script to execute online inference.\n\n```shell\n#!/bin/sh\n# this obtained through ifconfig\n# nic_name is the network interface name corresponding to local_ip of the current node\nnic_name=\"xxxx\"\nlocal_ip=\"xxxx\"\n\n# [Optional] jemalloc\n# jemalloc is for better performance, if `libjemalloc.so` is installed on your machine, you can turn it on.\n# export LD_PRELOAD=/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu/libjemalloc.so.2:$LD_PRELOAD\n\n# AIV\nexport HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE=\"AIV\"\n\nexport HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip\nexport GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport TP_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_BALANCE_SCHEDULING=1\nexport PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\n\nvllm serve /weights/DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot \\\n--host 0.0.0.0 \\\n--port 8015 \\\n--data-parallel-size 4 \\\n--tensor-parallel-size 4 \\\n--quantization ascend \\\n--seed 1024 \\\n--served-model-name deepseek_v3 \\\n--enable-expert-parallel \\\n--async-scheduling \\\n--max-num-seqs 16 \\\n--max-model-len 16384 \\\n--max-num-batched-tokens 4096 \\\n--trust-remote-code \\\n--no-enable-prefix-caching \\\n--gpu-memory-utilization 0.92 \\\n--speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 3, \"method\": \"mtp\"}' \\\n--compilation-config '{\"cudagraph_capture_sizes\":[4,16,32,48,64], \"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\"}'\n```\n\n**Notice:**\nThe parameters are explained as follows:\n\n- Setting the environment variable `VLLM_ASCEND_BALANCE_SCHEDULING=1` enables balance scheduling. This may help increase output throughput and reduce TPOT in v1 scheduler. However, TTFT may degrade in some scenarios. Furthermore, enabling this feature is not recommended in scenarios where PD is separated.\n- For single-node deployment, we recommend using `dp4tp4` instead of `dp2tp8`.\n- `--max-model-len` specifies the maximum context length - that is, the sum of input and output tokens for a single request. For performance testing with an input length of 3.5K and output length of 1.5K, a value of `16384` is sufficient, however, for precision testing, please set it at least `35000`.\n- `--no-enable-prefix-caching` indicates that prefix caching is disabled. To enable it, remove this option.\n- If you use the w4a8 weight, more memory will be allocated to kvcache, and you can try to increase system throughput to achieve greater throughput.\n\n### Multi-node Deployment\n\n- `DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot`: require at least 2 Atlas 800 A2 (64G × 8).\n\nRun the following scripts on two nodes respectively.\n\n**Node 0**\n\n```shell\n#!/bin/sh\n\n# this obtained through ifconfig\n# nic_name is the network interface name corresponding to local_ip of the current node\nnic_name=\"xxxx\"\nlocal_ip=\"xxxx\"\n\n# The value of node0_ip must be consistent with the value of local_ip set in node0 (master node)\nnode0_ip=\"xxxx\"\n\n# [Optional] jemalloc\n# jemalloc is for better performance, if `libjemalloc.so` is installed on your machine, you can turn it on.\n# export LD_PRELOAD=/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu/libjemalloc.so.2:$LD_PRELOAD\n\nexport HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip\nexport GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport TP_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport OMP_PROC_BIND=false\nexport OMP_NUM_THREADS=1\nexport HCCL_BUFFSIZE=200\nexport PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_BALANCE_SCHEDULING=1\nexport HCCL_INTRA_PCIE_ENABLE=1\nexport HCCL_INTRA_ROCE_ENABLE=0\n\nvllm serve [Mooncake](../features/pd_disaggregation_mooncake_multi_node.md).\n\nTake Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16) for example, we recommend to deploy 2P1D (4 nodes) rather than 1P1D (2 nodes), because there is no enough NPU memory to serve high concurrency in 1P1D case.\n\n- `DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot 2P1D Layerwise` require 4 Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16).\n\nTo run the vllm-ascend `Prefill-Decode Disaggregation` service, you need to deploy a `launch_online_dp.py` script and a `run_dp_template.sh` script on each node and deploy a `proxy.sh` script on prefill master node to forward requests.\n\n1. `launch_online_dp.py` to launch external dp vllm servers.\n[launch\\_online\\_dp.py](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm-ascend/blob/main/examples/external_online_dp/launch_online_dp.py)\n\n2. Prefill Node 0 `run_dp_template.sh` script\n\n    ```shell\n    # this obtained through ifconfig\n    # nic_name is the network interface name corresponding to local_ip of the current node\n --additional-config '{\"recompute_scheduler_enable\":true,\"multistream_overlap_shared_expert\": true,\"finegrained_tp_config\": {\"lmhead_tensor_parallel_size\":16}}' \\\n      --kv-transfer-config \\\n      '{\"kv_connector\": \"MooncakeConnectorV1\",\n      \"kv_role\": \"kv_consumer\",\n      \"kv_port\": \"30200\",\n      \"engine_id\": \"2\",\n      \"kv_connector_extra_config\": {\n                \"prefill\": {\n                        \"dp_size\": 2,\n                        \"tp_size\": 8\n                },\n                \"decode\": {\n                        \"dp_size\": 32,\n                        \"tp_size\": 1\n                }\n          }\n      }'\n    ```\n\n    **Notice:**\n    The parameters are explained as follows:\n\n    - `VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_FLASHCOMM1=1`: enables the communication optimization function on the prefill nodes.\n    - `VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_MLAPO=1`: enables the fusion operator, which can significantly improve performance but consumes more NPU memory. In the Prefill-Decode (PD) separation scenario, enable MLAPO only on decode nodes.\n    - `--async-scheduling`: enables the asynchronous scheduling function. When Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) is enabled, asynchronous scheduling of operator delivery can be implemented to overlap the operator delivery latency.\n    - `cudagraph_capture_sizes`: The recommended value is `n x (mtp + 1)`. And the min is `n = 1` and the max is `n = max-num-seqs`. For other values, it is recommended to set them to the number of frequently occurring requests on the Decode (D) node.\n    - `recompute_scheduler_enable: true`: enables the recomputation scheduler. When the Key-Value Cache (KV Cache) of the decode node is insufficient, requests will be sent to the prefill node to recompute the KV Cache. In the PD separation scenario, it is recommended to enable this configuration on both prefill and decode nodes simultaneously.\n    - `multistream_overlap_shared_expert: true`: When the Tensor Parallelism (TP) size is 1 or `enable_shared_expert_dp: true`, an additional stream is enabled to overlap the computation process of shared experts for improved efficiency.\n    - `lmhead_tensor_parallel_size: 16`: When the Tensor Parallelism (TP) size of the decode node is 1, this parameter allows the TP size of the LMHead embedding layer to be greater than 1, which is used to reduce the computational load of each card on the LMHead embedding layer.\n\n6. run server for each node:\n\n    ```shell\n    # p0\n    python launch_online_dp.py --dp-size 2 --tp-size 8 --dp-size-local 2 --dp-rank-start 0 --dp-address 141.xx.xx.1 --dp-rpc-port 12321 --vllm-start-port 7100\n    # p1\n    python launch_online_dp.py --dp-size 2 --tp-size 8 --dp-size-local 2 --dp-rank-start 0 --dp-address 141.xx.xx.2 --dp-rpc-port 12321 --vllm-start-port 7100\n    # d0\n    python launch_online_dp.py --dp-size 32 --tp-size 1 --dp-size-local 16 --dp-rank-start 0 --dp-address 141.xx.xx.3 --dp-rpc-port 12321 --vllm-start-port 7100\n    # d1\n    python launch_online_dp.py --dp-size 32 --tp-size 1 --dp-size-local 16 --dp-rank-start 16 --dp-address 141.xx.xx.3 --dp-rpc-port 12321 --vllm-start-port 7100\n    ```\n\n7. Compared to the previous version, this upgrade brings improvements in multiple aspects:\n\n- Hybrid thinking mode: One model supports both thinking mode and non-thinking mode by changing the chat template.\n\n- Smarter tool calling: Through post-training optimization, the model's performance in tool usage and agent tasks has significantly improved.\n\n- Higher thinking efficiency: DeepSeek-V3.1-Think achieves comparable answer quality to DeepSeek-R1-0528, while responding more quickly.\n\nThe `DeepSeek-V3.1` model is first supported in `vllm-ascend:v0.9.1rc3`.\n\nThis document will show the main verification steps of the model, including supported features, feature configuration, environment preparation, single-node and multi-node deployment, accuracy and performance evaluation.\n\n## Supported Features\n\nRefer to [supported features](../../user_guide/support_matrix/supported_models.md) to get the model's supported feature matrix.\n\nRefer to [feature guide](../../user_guide/feature_guide/index.md) to get the feature's configuration.\n\n## Environment Preparation\n\n### Model Weight\n\n- `DeepSeek-V3.1`(BF16 version): [Download model weight](https://www.modelscope.cn/models/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V3.1).\n- `DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot`(Quantized version with mix mtp): [Download model weight](https://www.modelscope.cn/models/Eco-Tech/DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot).\n- `DeepSeek-V3.1-Terminus-w4a8-mtp-QuaRot`(Quantized version with mix mtp): [Download model weight](https://www.modelscope.cn/models/Eco-Tech/DeepSeek-V3.1-Terminus-w4a8-mtp-QuaRot).\n- `Quantization method`: [msmodelslim](https://gitcode.com/Ascend/msit/blob/master/msmodelslim/example/DeepSeek/README.md#deepseek-v31-w8a8-%E6%B7%B7%E5%90%88%E9%87%8F%E5%8C%96-mtp-%E9%87%8F%E5%8C%96). You can use this method to quantize the model.\n\nIt is recommended to download the model weight to the shared directory of multiple nodes, such as `/root/.cache/`.\n\n### Verify Multi-node Communication(Optional)\n\nIf you want to deploy multi-node environment, you need to verify multi-node communication according to [verify multi-node communication environment](../../installation.md#verify-multi-node-communication).\n\n### Installation\n\nYou can use our official docker image to run `DeepSeek-V3.1` directly.\n\nSelect an image based on your machine type and start the docker image on your node, refer to [using docker](../../installation.md#set-up-using-docker).\n\n```{code-block} bash\n   :substitutions:\n# Update --device according to your device (Atlas A2: /dev/davinci[0-7] Atlas A3:/dev/davinci[0-15]).\n# Update the vllm-ascend image according to your environment.\n# Note you should download the weight to /root/.cache in advance.\n# Update the vllm-ascend image\nexport IMAGE=m.daocloud.io/quay.io/ascend/vllm-ascend:|vllm_ascend_version|\nexport NAME=vllm-ascend\n\n# Run the container using the defined variables\n# Note: If you are running bridge network with docker, please expose available ports for multiple nodes communication in advance.\ndocker run --rm \\\n--name $NAME \\\n--net=host \\\n--shm-size=1g \\\n--device /dev/davinci0 \\\n--device /dev/davinci1 \\\n--device /dev/davinci2 \\\n--device /dev/davinci3 \\\n--device /dev/davinci4 \\\n--device /dev/davinci5 \\\n--device /dev/davinci6 \\\n--device /dev/davinci7 \\\n--device /dev/davinci_manager \\\n--device /dev/devmm_svm \\\n--device /dev/hisi_hdc \\\n-v /usr/local/dcmi:/usr/local/dcmi \\\n-v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/tools/hccn_tool:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/tools/hccn_tool \\\n-v /usr/local/bin/npu-smi:/usr/local/bin/npu-smi \\\n-v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/lib64/:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/lib64/ \\\n-v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/version.info:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/version.info \\\n-v /etc/ascend_install.info:/etc/ascend_install.info \\\n-v /root/.cache:/root/.cache \\\n-it $IMAGE bash\n```\n\nIf you want to deploy multi-node environment, you need to set up environment on each node.\n\n## Deployment\n\n### Single-node Deployment\n\n- Quantized model `DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot` can be deployed on 1 Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16).\n\nRun the following script to execute online inference.\n\n```shell\n#!/bin/sh\n# this obtained through ifconfig\n# nic_name is the network interface name corresponding to local_ip of the current node\nnic_name=\"xxxx\"\nlocal_ip=\"xxxx\"\n\n# [Optional] jemalloc\n# jemalloc is for better performance, if `libjemalloc.so` is installed on your machine, you can turn it on.\n# export LD_PRELOAD=/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu/libjemalloc.so.2:$LD_PRELOAD\n\n# AIV\nexport HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE=\"AIV\"\n\nexport HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip\nexport GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport TP_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_BALANCE_SCHEDULING=1\nexport PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\n\nvllm serve /weights/DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot \\\n--host 0.0.0.0 \\\n--port 8015 \\\n--data-parallel-size 4 \\\n--tensor-parallel-size 4 \\\n--quantization ascend \\\n--seed 1024 \\\n--served-model-name deepseek_v3 \\\n--enable-expert-parallel \\\n--async-scheduling \\\n--max-num-seqs 16 \\\n--max-model-len 16384 \\\n--max-num-batched-tokens 4096 \\\n--trust-remote-code \\\n--no-enable-prefix-caching \\\n--gpu-memory-utilization 0.92 \\\n--speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 3, \"method\": \"mtp\"}' \\\n--compilation-config '{\"cudagraph_capture_sizes\":[4,16,32,48,64], \"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\"}'\n```\n\n**Notice:**\nThe parameters are explained as follows:\n\n- Setting the environment variable `VLLM_ASCEND_BALANCE_SCHEDULING=1` enables balance scheduling. This may help increase output throughput and reduce TPOT in v1 scheduler. However, TTFT may degrade in some scenarios. Furthermore, enabling this feature is not recommended in scenarios where PD is separated.\n- For single-node deployment, we recommend using `dp4tp4` instead of `dp2tp8`.\n- `--max-model-len` specifies the maximum context length - that is, the sum of input and output tokens for a single request. For performance testing with an input length of 3.5K and output length of 1.5K, a value of `16384` is sufficient, however, for precision testing, please set it at least `35000`.\n- `--no-enable-prefix-caching` indicates that prefix caching is disabled. To enable it, remove this option.\n- If you use the w4a8 weight, more memory will be allocated to kvcache, and you can try to increase system throughput to achieve greater throughput.\n\n### Multi-node Deployment\n\n- `DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot`: require at least 2 Atlas 800 A2 (64G × 8).\n\nRun the following scripts on two nodes respectively.\n\n**Node 0**\n\n```shell\n#!/bin/sh\n\n# this obtained through ifconfig\n# nic_name is the network interface name corresponding to local_ip of the current node\nnic_name=\"xxxx\"\nlocal_ip=\"xxxx\"\n\n# The value of node0_ip must be consistent with the value of local_ip set in node0 (master node)\nnode0_ip=\"xxxx\"\n\n# [Optional] jemalloc\n# jemalloc is for better performance, if `libjemalloc.so` is installed on your machine, you can turn it on.\n# export LD_PRELOAD=/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu/libjemalloc.so.2:$LD_PRELOAD\n\nexport HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip\nexport GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport 13389 \\\n--tensor-parallel-size 4 \\\n--quantization ascend \\\n--seed 1024 \\\n--served-model-name deepseek_v3 \\\n--enable-expert-parallel \\\n--async-scheduling \\\n--max-num-seqs 16 \\\n--max-model-len 16384 \\\n--max-num-batched-tokens 4096 \\\n--trust-remote-code \\\n--no-enable-prefix-caching \\\n--gpu-memory-utilization 0.92 \\\n--speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 3, \"method\": \"mtp\"}' \\\n--compilation-config '{\"cudagraph_capture_sizes\":[4,16,32,48,64], \"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\"}'\n```\n\n**Node \"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\"}'\n```\n\n### Prefill-Decode Disaggregation\n\nWe recommend using Mooncake for deployment: [Mooncake](../features/pd_disaggregation_mooncake_multi_node.md).\n\nTake Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16) for example, we recommend to deploy 2P1D (4 nodes) rather than 1P1D (2 nodes), because there is no enough NPU memory to serve high concurrency in 1P1D case.\n\n- `DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot 2P1D Layerwise` require 4 Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16).\n\nTo run the vllm-ascend `Prefill-Decode Disaggregation` service, you need to deploy a `launch_online_dp.py` script and a `run_dp_template.sh` script on each node and deploy a `proxy.sh` script on prefill master node to forward requests.\n\n1. `launch_online_dp.py` to launch external dp vllm servers.\n[launch\\_online\\_dp.py](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm-ascend/blob/main/examples/external_online_dp/launch_online_dp.py)\n\n2. Prefill Node 0 `run_dp_template.sh` script\n\n    ```shell\n    # this obtained through ifconfig\n    # nic_name is the network interface name corresponding to local_ip of the current node\n nic_name=\"xxx\"\n    local_ip=\"141.xx.xx.4\"\n\n    # The value of node0_ip must be consistent with the value of local_ip set in node0 (master node)\n    node0_ip=\"xxxx\"\n\n    # [Optional] jemalloc\n    # jemalloc is for better performance, if `libjemalloc.so` is installed on your machine, you can turn it on.\n    # export LD_PRELOAD=/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu/libjemalloc.so.2:$LD_PRELOAD\n\n    export HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip\n    export GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\n    export TP_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\n    export HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\n\n    export VLLM_RPC_TIMEOUT=3600000\n    export VLLM_EXECUTE_MODEL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=30000\n    export HCCL_EXEC_TIMEOUT=204\n    export HCCL_CONNECT_TIMEOUT=120\n\n    export OMP_PROC_BIND=false\n    export OMP_NUM_THREADS=10\n    export PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\n    export HCCL_BUFFSIZE=1100\n    export TASK_QUEUE_ENABLE=1\n    export HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE=\"AIV\"\n    export VLLM_USE_V1=1\n    export ASCEND_RT_VISIBLE_DEVICES=$1\n    export ASCEND_BUFFER_POOL=4:8\n    export LD_LIBRARY_PATH=/usr/local/Ascend/ascend-toolkit/latest/python/site-packages/mooncake:$LD_LIBRARY_PATH\n\n    vllm serve /weights/DeepSeek-V3.1-w8a8-mtp-QuaRot \\\n      --host 0.0.0.0 \\\n      --port $2 \\\n      --data-parallel-size $3 \\\n      --data-parallel-rank $4 \\\n      --data-parallel-address $5 \\\n      --data-parallel-rpc-port $6 \\\n      --tensor-parallel-size $7 \\\n      --enable-expert-parallel \\\n      --seed 1024 \\\n      --served-model-name deepseek_v3 \\\n      --max-model-len 65536 \\\n      --max-num-batched-tokens 256 \\\n      --max-num-seqs 28 \\\n      --trust-remote-code \\\n      --gpu-memory-utilization 0.92 \\\n      --quantization ascend \\\n      --no-enable-prefix-caching \\\n      --async-scheduling \\\n      --speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 1, \"method\": \"mtp\"}' \\\n      --compilation-config '{\"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\", \"cudagraph_capture_sizes\":[2, 4, 8, 16, 24, 32, 48, 56]}' \\\n      --additional-config '{\"recompute_scheduler_enable\":true,\"multistream_overlap_shared_expert\": true,\"finegrained_tp_config\": {\"lmhead_tensor_parallel_size\":16}}' \\\n      --kv-transfer-config \\\n      '{\"kv_connector\": \"MooncakeConnectorV1\",\n      \"kv_role\": \"kv_consumer\",\n      \"kv_port\": \"30200\",\n      \"engine_id\": \"2\",\n      \"kv_connector_extra_config\": {\n                \"prefill\": {\n                        \"dp_size\": 2,\n                        \"tp_size\": 8\n                },\n                \"decode\": {\n                        \"dp_size\": 32,\n                        \"tp_size\": 1\n                }\n          }\n      }'\n    ```\n\n    **Notice:**\n    The parameters are explained as follows:\n\n    - `VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_FLASHCOMM1=1`: enables the communication optimization function on the prefill nodes.\n    - `VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_MLAPO=1`: enables the fusion operator, which can significantly improve performance but consumes more NPU memory. In the Prefill-Decode (PD) separation scenario, enable MLAPO only on decode nodes.\n    - `--async-scheduling`: enables the asynchronous scheduling function. When Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) is enabled, asynchronous scheduling of operator delivery can be implemented to overlap the operator delivery latency.\n    - `cudagraph_capture_sizes`: The recommended value is `n x (mtp + 1)`. And the min is `n = 1` and the max is `n = max-num-seqs`. For other values, it is recommended to set them to the number of frequently occurring requests on the Decode (D) node.\n    - `recompute_scheduler_enable: true`: enables the recomputation scheduler. When the Key-Value Cache (KV Cache) of the decode node is insufficient, requests will be sent to the prefill node to recompute the KV Cache. In the PD separation scenario, it is recommended to enable this configuration on both prefill and decode nodes simultaneously.\n    - `multistream_overlap_shared_expert: true`: When the Tensor Parallelism (TP) size is 1 or `enable_shared_expert_dp: true`, an additional stream is enabled to overlap the computation process of shared experts for improved efficiency.\n    - `lmhead_tensor_parallel_size: 16`: When the Tensor Parallelism (TP) size of the decode node is 1, this parameter allows the TP size of the LMHead embedding layer to be greater than 1, which is used to reduce the computational load of each card on the LMHead embedding layer.\n\n6. run server for each node:\n\n    ```shell\n    # p0\n    python launch_online_dp.py --dp-size 2 --tp-size 8 --dp-size-local 2 --dp-rank-start 0 --dp-address 141.xx.xx.1 --dp-rpc-port 12321 --vllm-start-port 7100\n    # p1\n    python launch_online_dp.py --dp-size 2 --tp-size 8 --dp-size-local 2 --dp-rank-start 0 --dp-address 141.xx.xx.2 --dp-rpc-port 12321 --vllm-start-port 7100\n    # d0\n    python launch_online_dp.py --dp-size 32 --tp-size 1 --dp-size-local 16 --dp-rank-start 0 --dp-address 141.xx.xx.3 --dp-rpc-port 12321 --vllm-start-port 7100\n    # d1\n    python launch_online_dp.py --dp-size 32 --tp-size 1 --dp-size-local 16 --dp-rank-start 16 --dp-address 141.xx.xx.3 --dp-rpc-port 12321 --vllm-start-port 7100\n    ```\n\n7. Run the `proxy.sh` script on the prefill master node\n\n    Run a proxy server on the same node with the prefiller service instance. 141.xx.xx.3 \\\n        141.xx.xx.3 \\\n        141.xx.xx.3 \\\n        141.xx.xx.3 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n        141.xx.xx.4 \\\n      --decoder-ports \\\n        7100 7101 7102 7103 7104 7105 7106 7107 7108 7109 7110 7111 7112 7113 7114 7115 \\\n        7100 7101 7102 7103 7104 7105 7106 7107 7108 7109 7110 7111 7112 7113 7114 7115 \\\n    ```\n\n    ```shell\n    cd vllm-ascend/examples/disaggregated_prefill_v1/\n    bash proxy.sh\n
